S1's files (everything new since branch `s1-foundation`) are on disk and committed — build on top of them.

**Locked decisions (do not relitigate during plan/critique):**
1. **Dual-key transition stays dual.** Coalescer and newer-inbound suppression service BOTH `(user_id)` and `(user_id, bot_id)` keys for the duration of S2a. Legacy reader paths are retired in S2b, not here.

## Sprint 2a — §16.2 verbatim

**Goal.** Insert sites write the new columns; coalescer and newer-inbound suppression are rekeyed with a dual-key transition; per-bot observability comes online; pre-S3 eval baseline captured. Mediator behavior unchanged. **No NOT NULL.**

**Pre-flight context.**

- Confirm S1 backfill held — query `SELECT count(*) FROM mediator.artifact_topics` (must be 235 from S1; no new writes yet).
- Exhaustive grep sweep of insert sites, broader than the obvious ones: `inbound.py:161`, `messaging.py:55, 84`, `agentic.py:483`, `agentic.py:577` (deferred jobs), `scheduled_jobs.py:216` (scheduler seeds), `write_tools.py:1367` (scheduled-task writes), every `INSERT INTO memories|themes|observations|watch_items|distillations|out_of_bounds` site. Checklist file local to the sprint.

**Work items, ordered.**

1. Wire `routing.py` into `inbound.py:23-180`. Populate `TurnContext` with real scope fields (still mediator-only defaults).
2. Stamp insert sites:
   - `inbound.py:161` — `bot_id`, `topic_id`, `channel_id`.
   - `messaging.py:55, 84` — same on outbound.
   - `agentic.py:483` — `bot_id`, `topic_id`, `bot_spec_version` (hash of `BotSpec` repr), `hot_context_builder_version`, `tool_schema_version` (hash of `tool_schemas.py`).
   - `agentic.py:577` deferred jobs and `scheduled_jobs.py:216` scheduler seeds — stamp `bot_id`, `topic_id`.
   - `write_tools.py:1367` scheduled-task writes — same.
   - Every artifact write in `write_tools.py`: stamp `recorded_by_bot_id` AND create matching `artifact_topics` row(s) for the bot's primary topic in the same transaction.
3. **Dual-key burst coalescer** (`app/services/debouncer.py`). In-memory dicts keyed by `user_id` today; new code services BOTH `(user_id)` and `(user_id, bot_id)` keys during this sprint. Bursts in-flight at deploy flush under old key; new bursts use the composite key. Drop the legacy reader in S2b.
4. **Dual-key newer-inbound suppression**:
   - `agentic.py:612` (final-send suppression).
   - `read_tools.py:166` (incremental-send suppression).
   - Filter: `(bot_id = ctx.bot_id OR bot_id IS NULL)` during transition — the `IS NULL` half catches legacy boundary rows written before the stamping deploy.
5. Scheduled-job dispatcher (`scheduled_jobs.py`) reads `bot_id` from row. No-op with one bot but the code path is live.
6. **Per-bot observability**: every log line and structured event in inbound, agentic, messaging, scheduler, OOB check carries `bot_id`, `topic_id`, `channel_id`, `binding_id`. Dashboards split by bot (even though only one bot exists yet — the panes are there for S5).
7. **Per-(user, bot) pause read path**: outbound and scheduler consult `user_bot_state.paused` in addition to `system_state.is_paused`. Write path for per-(user, bot) pause stays deferred. Global pause remains the kill switch.
8. **Eval baseline**: run the existing mediator eval suite against current prod-like state and snapshot results. This is the pre-S3 regression reference.
9. Tests/fixtures take a `bot_spec` argument (default mediator).
10. **Lint**: CI warns (does NOT fail) on any new artifact INSERT outside a transaction that also creates the `artifact_topics` row, and on any new `INSERT INTO messages|bot_turns|scheduled_jobs|feedback` missing `bot_id`/`topic_id`.

Dependencies: (1) gates (2–7); (8) runs against pre-stamp prod, so day 1.

**Definition of done.**

- All insert sites stamp the new columns. Coalescer + suppression rekeyed with dual-key transition live. Per-bot observability live. Per-(user, bot) pause read-path live (write-path deferred). Eval baseline captured. **No NOT NULL constraints yet.**
- Full pytest green, no regressions.
- Every new INSERT statement either includes `bot_id`/`topic_id` or is flagged by the lint (advisory).

## S1 lessons the executor must honor

1. **Before declaring any new script/CLI complete**: actually invoke it with `--help` (or equivalent entry point) to confirm it parses and basic argparse setup works. Pytest passing is necessary but not sufficient — pytest may bypass `main()`.
2. **Any SQL fragment in a Python string**: dry-run via `EXPLAIN` against the live schema, or sanity-check the aggregate/operator types against actual column types (e.g. `max(uuid)` doesn't exist in Postgres).
3. **Any asyncpg connection** against the configured `DATABASE_URL`: pass `statement_cache_size=0` — Supabase transaction-mode pooler (port 6543) is what's configured. Without this flag, scripts crash on first prepared-statement reuse.
4. **No `hash()` for deterministic IDs**: Python's `hash()` is salted per-process. Use `hashlib.sha1` for advisory-lock-id derivation or anything needing cross-run determinism.
5. **Schema filters in validation queries**: production tables live in schema `mediator`, NOT `public`. `information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = 'mediator'`.

## Stop conditions

- Any test that was passing on `main` starts failing — STOP, report.
- Any work item's executor decides the design is wrong and tries to redesign — STOP, report.
- Schema migration would touch existing rows destructively — STOP, report.
- Any artifact INSERT path can't fit `artifact_topics` write in the same transaction — STOP, report.

````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

1	"""Routing helpers — resolve bots, senders, and bindings.
2	
3	NOT wired into inbound.py yet. Sprint 1 delivers the module + unit tests.
4	Sprint 2a wires resolve_bot into the inbound path.
5	"""
6	
7	from __future__ import annotations
8	
9	from typing import Any, NamedTuple
10	from uuid import UUID
11	
12	
13	class BindingResolution(NamedTuple):
14	    binding_id: UUID
15	    bot_id: str
16	    dyad_id: UUID | None
17	    user_id: UUID | None
18	
19	
20	async def resolve_bot(pool: Any, *, transport: str, address: str) -> str | None:
21	    """Map a transport+address pair to a bot_id via the channels table.
22	
23	    Returns the bot_id of the channel that matches, or None if no channel found.
24	    """
25	    row = await pool.fetchrow(
26	        """
27	        SELECT bot_id
28	        FROM channels
29	        WHERE transport = $1 AND address = $2
30	        LIMIT 1
31	        """,
32	        transport,
33	        address,
34	    )
35	    return row["bot_id"] if row is not None else None
36	
37	
38	async def resolve_sender(pool: Any, *, transport: str, address: str) -> UUID | None:
39	    """Map a transport+address pair to a user_id via user_identities.
40	
41	    Returns the user_id, or None if the identity is not registered.
42	    """
43	    row = await pool.fetchrow(
44	        """
45	        SELECT user_id
46	        FROM user_identities
47	        WHERE transport = $1 AND address = $2
48	        """,
49	        transport,
50	        address,
51	    )
52	    return row["user_id"] if row is not None else None
53	
54	
55	async def resolve_binding(pool: Any, *, bot_id: str, user_id: UUID) -> BindingResolution | None:
56	    """Find the binding between a bot and a user.
57	
58	    Looks up bot_bindings joined with dyad_members to find the binding
59	    that links this bot to this user (either directly via user_id or through
60	    dyad membership). Returns the BindingResolution or None.
61	
62	    The ORDER BY makes the result deterministic if multiple bindings ever
63	    match — pick the oldest (earliest created_at) and break ties on id.
64	    """
65	    row = await pool.fetchrow(
66	        """
67	        SELECT bb.id    AS binding_id,
68	               bb.bot_id,
69	               bb.dyad_id,
70	               bb.user_id
71	        FROM bot_bindings bb
72	        LEFT JOIN dyad_members dm ON dm.dyad_id = bb.dyad_id
73	        WHERE bb.bot_id = $1
74	          AND (bb.user_id = $2 OR dm.user_id = $2)
75	        ORDER BY bb.created_at, bb.id
76	        LIMIT 1
77	        """,
78	        bot_id,
79	        user_id,
80	    )
81	    if row is None:
82	        return None
83	    return BindingResolution(
84	        binding_id=row["binding_id"],
85	        bot_id=row["bot_id"],
86	        dyad_id=row["dyad_id"],
87	        user_id=row["user_id"],
88	    )
```

50	async def partner_of(pool: Any, user: User) -> User:
51	    rows = await pool.fetch(

53	        SELECT id, name, phone, timezone
54	        FROM users
55	        WHERE id <> $1
56	        """,
57	        user.id,
58	    )
59	    if len(rows) != 1:
60	        raise ValueError(f"expected exactly one partner for user {user.id}, found {len(rows)}")
61	    row = rows[0]
62	    return User(id=row["id"], name=row["name"], phone=row["phone"], timezone=row["timezone"])

41	    return phone

44	def _delete_target_id(value: dict[str, Any], message: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
45	    if message.get("type") != "unsupported":
46	        return None
47	    for error in value.get("errors", []):
48	        if error.get("code") == 131051:
49	            return error.get("message_id") or message.get("context", {}).get("message_id") or message.get("id")
50	    return None

53	def _edit_target_id(message: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
54	    if message.get("type") != "text":
55	        return None
56	    return message.get("context", {}).get("message_id")

85	        return "positive"
86	    if emoji == "👎":
87	        return "negative"
88	    return "mixed"

116	async def _control_recipients(pool: Any, user) -> list[Any]:

118	    try:

120	    except ValueError:

147	async def _insert_message(
148	    pool: Any,
149	    user_id,
150	    content: str | None,
151	    wa_id: str,
152	    sent_at: datetime,
153	    media_type: str | None = None,
154	    media_url: str | None = None,
155	    duration: int | None = None,
156	    media_analysis: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
157	    charge: str | None = None,
158	):
159	    return await pool.fetchrow(
160	        """
161	        INSERT INTO messages
162	            (direction, sender_id, content, content_encrypted, processing_state, whatsapp_message_id, sent_at,
163	             media_type, media_url, media_duration_seconds, media_analysis, charge)
164	        VALUES ('inbound', $1, $2, $3, 'raw', $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9, $10)
165	        ON CONFLICT (whatsapp_message_id) DO NOTHING
166	        RETURNING id
167	        """,
168	        user_id,
169	        content,
170	        encrypt_value(content),
171	        wa_id,
172	        sent_at,
173	        media_type,
174	        media_url,
175	        duration,
176	        media_analysis,
177	        charge,
178	    )
179	
180	
181	async def process_inbound(
182	    pool: Any,
183	    payload: dict[str, Any],
184	    coalescer: Any | None = None,
185	    *,
186	    coalescer_source: str = "live",
187	) -> None:
188	    for entry in payload.get("entry", []):
189	        for change in entry.get("changes", []):
190	            value = change.get("value", {})
191	            # Process image messages first within a single inbound value so that
192	            # vision.handle_image populates media_analysis before any text-driven
193	            # coalescer add fires. This keeps a Discord/WhatsApp message that
194	            # bundles text + image attachments to a single agentic turn that
195	            # already knows about the image, instead of racing into two replies.
196	            messages_in_value = list(value.get("messages", []))
197	            messages_in_value.sort(key=lambda m: 0 if m.get("type") == "image" else 1)
198	            for message in messages_in_value:
199	                delete_target = _delete_target_id(value, message)
200	                if delete_target is not None:
201	                    await _handle_delete(pool, delete_target)
202	                    continue
203	
204	                edit_target = _edit_target_id(message)
205	                if edit_target is not None:
206	                    await _handle_edit(pool, edit_target, message["text"]["body"])
207	                    continue
208	
209	                phone = message["from"]
210	                if not is_allowed_phone(phone):
211	                    logger.warning("dropping non-whitelisted sender %s", phone)
212	                    continue
213	
214	                user = await upsert_user(
215	                    pool,
216	                    _contact_name(value, phone),
217	                    phone,
218	                    get_settings().default_user_timezone,
219	                )
220	                wa_type = message["type"]
221	                sent_at = _parse_sent_at(message)
222	                wa_id = message["id"]
223	
224	                if wa_type == "reaction":
225	                    await _handle_reaction(pool, user.id, message.get("reaction", {}))
226	                    continue
227	
228	                if wa_type == "text":
229	                    content = message["text"]["body"]
230	                    if content.strip() in {"/pause", "/resume"}:
231	                        charge_label = "routine"
232	                    else:
233	                        charge_label = (await classify_charge(pool, content)).charge
234	                    row = await _insert_message(pool, user.id, content, wa_id, sent_at, charge=charge_label)
235	                    if row is not None and content.strip() == "/pause":
236	                        await _handle_pause_command(pool, user)
237	                        continue
238	                    if row is not None and content.strip() == "/resume":
239	                        await _handle_resume_command(pool, user)
240	                        continue
241	                    if row is not None and await system_state.is_paused(pool):
242	                        continue
243	                    if row is not None and coalescer is not None and not await system_state.is_paused(pool):
244	                        await coalescer.add(user.id, row["id"], user, source=coalescer_source)
245	                    continue
246	
247	                if wa_type == "audio":
248	                    duration = message["audio"].get("duration")
249	                    row = await _insert_message(pool, user.id, None, wa_id, sent_at, "voice", duration=duration)
250	                    if row is not None:
251	                        paused = await system_state.is_paused(pool)
252	                        if not paused and await claim_onboarding_welcome(pool, user.id):
253	                            await send_outbound(pool, user, WELCOME_MESSAGE)
254	                        await handle_voice(pool, row["id"], message["audio"]["id"], user, None if paused else coalescer, duration)
255	                    continue
256	
257	                if wa_type == "image":
258	                    row = await _insert_message(pool, user.id, None, wa_id, sent_at, "image")
259	                    if row is not None:
260	                        paused = await system_state.is_paused(pool)
261	                        if not paused and await claim_onboarding_welcome(pool, user.id):
262	                            await send_outbound(pool, user, WELCOME_MESSAGE)
263	                        await handle_image(pool, row["id"], message["image"]["id"], user, None if paused else coalescer)
264	                    continue
265	
266	                row = await _insert_message(
267	                    pool,
268	                    user.id,
269	                    None,
270	                    wa_id,
271	                    sent_at,
272	                    "document",
273	                    media_analysis={"kind": wa_type},
274	                )
275	                if row is not None and await system_state.is_paused(pool):
276	                    continue
277	                if row is not None and await claim_onboarding_welcome(pool, user.id):
278	                    await send_outbound(pool, user, WELCOME_MESSAGE)
279	                if row is not None and coalescer is not None and not await system_state.is_paused(pool):
280	                    await coalescer.add(user.id, row["id"], user, source=coalescer_source)
281	
282	
283	def twilio_form_to_meta_payload(form: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
284	    """Convert Twilio's application/x-www-form-urlencoded webhook to our Meta-shaped ingester."""
285	    from_value = form.get("From", "")
286	    phone = from_value.removeprefix("whatsapp:")
287	    wa_id = form.get("MessageSid") or form.get("SmsMessageSid") or str(uuid5(NAMESPACE_URL, repr(sorted(form.items()))))
288	    body = form.get("Body", "")
289	    num_media = int(form.get("NumMedia") or "0")
290	    profile_name = form.get("ProfileName") or phone
291	
292	    if num_media > 0:
293	        content_type = form.get("MediaContentType0", "")
294	        media_url = form.get("MediaUrl0", "")
295	        if content_type.startswith("image/"):
296	            message = {"from": phone, "id": wa_id, "timestamp": str(int(datetime.now(UTC).timestamp())), "type": "image", "image": {"id": media_url}}
297	        elif content_type.startswith("audio/"):
298	            message = {"from": phone, "id": wa_id, "timestamp": str(int(datetime.now(UTC).timestamp())), "type": "audio", "audio": {"id": media_url}}
299	        else:
300	            message = {"from": phone, "id": wa_id, "timestamp": str(int(datetime.now(UTC).timestamp())), "type": "document"}
301	    else:
302	        message = {"from": phone, "id": wa_id, "timestamp": str(int(datetime.now(UTC).timestamp())), "type": "text", "text": {"body": body}}
303	
304	    return {
305	        "entry": [
306	            {
307	                "changes": [
308	                    {
309	                        "value": {
310	                            "contacts": [{"wa_id": phone, "profile": {"name": profile_name}}],
311	                            "messages": [message],
312	                        }
313	                    }
314	                ]
315	            }
316	        ]
317	    }
318
```

42	async def _insert_outbound(
43	    pool: Any,
44	    user: User,
45	    content: str,
46	    state: str = "raw",
47	    *,
48	    bot_turn_id: UUID | None = None,

52	    if bot_turn_id is not None or outbound_part_key is not None or outbound_part_index is not None:
53	        row = await pool.fetchrow(
54	            """
55	            INSERT INTO messages (
56	                direction, recipient_id, content, content_encrypted, processing_state, sent_at,
57	                bot_turn_id, outbound_part_key, outbound_part_index
58	            )
59	            VALUES ('outbound', $1, $2, $3, $4, now(), $5, $6, $7)
60	            ON CONFLICT (outbound_part_key) DO NOTHING
61	            RETURNING id
62	            """,

65	            encrypt_value(content),

71	        if row is not None:
72	            return row["id"]
73	        if outbound_part_key is None:
74	            raise RuntimeError("outbound insert unexpectedly conflicted without a part key")
75	        existing = await pool.fetchrow(
76	            "SELECT id FROM messages WHERE outbound_part_key=$1",
77	            outbound_part_key,
78	        )
79	        if existing is None:
80	            raise RuntimeError("outbound part conflict without an existing row")
81	        return existing["id"]
82	    row = await pool.fetchrow(
83	        """
84	        INSERT INTO messages (direction, recipient_id, content, content_encrypted, processing_state, sent_at)
85	        VALUES ('outbound', $1, $2, $3, $4, now())

214	            verdict=verdict["verdict"],

216	        )
217	        return {

283	
284	async def _send_with_retry(send_call) -> dict[str, Any]:
285	    last_error: Exception | None = None
286	    max_attempts = 3
287	    for attempt in range(max_attempts):
288	        try:
289	            return await send_call()
290	        except Exception as exc:
291	            last_error = exc
292	            if attempt == max_attempts - 1:
293	                break
294	            await asyncio.sleep(_RETRY_BACKOFF_SECONDS[attempt])
295	    raise last_error  # type: ignore[misc]
296	
297	
298	async def _call_oob_hook(
299	    pool: Any,
300	    content: str,
301	    recipient_id: UUID,
302	    protected_owner_ids: list[UUID] | None = None,
303	) -> dict[str, Any]:
304	    hook = hooks.check_oob
305	    if hook is None:
306	        return {"verdict": "ok", "reason": "OOB hook disabled", "suggested_rewrite": None, "checker_failed": False}
307	    try:
308	        verdict = await hook(pool, content, recipient_id, protected_owner_ids=protected_owner_ids)
309	    except TypeError:
310	        try:
311	            verdict = await hook(pool, content, recipient_id)
312	        except TypeError:
313	            verdict = await hook(content, recipient_id)
314	    if hasattr(verdict, "model_dump"):
315	        verdict = verdict.model_dump(mode="json")
316	    if "suggested_rewrite" not in verdict and "rewrite" in verdict:
317	        verdict["suggested_rewrite"] = verdict.get("rewrite")
318	    verdict.setdefault("checker_failed", False)
319	    verdict.setdefault("reason", "")
320	    return verdict
321	
322	
323	async def send_outbound(
324	    pool: Any,
325	    user: User,
326	    content: str,
327	    *,
328	    template_fallback: TemplateCall | None = None,
329	    bot_turn_id: UUID | None = None,
330	    ignore_pause: bool = False,
331	    protected_owner_ids: list[UUID] | None = None,
332	    send_typing_indicator: bool = True,
333	    before_provider_send: Callable[[], Awaitable[None]] | None = None,
334	) -> UUID:
335	    if not ignore_pause and (await system_state.is_paused(pool) or await hooks.paused_for_user(user.id)):
336	        return await _insert_outbound(pool, user, content, "withheld", bot_turn_id=bot_turn_id)
337	
338	    verdict = await _call_oob_hook(pool, content, user.id, protected_owner_ids)
339	    if verdict["verdict"] == "block":
340	        await _append_turn_reasoning(pool, bot_turn_id, f"Outbound blocked by OOB hook: {verdict['reason']}")
341	        row_id = await _insert_outbound(pool, user, content, "withheld", bot_turn_id=bot_turn_id)
342	        await record_withheld_outbound_review(
343	            pool,
344	            recipient_id=user.id,
345	            outbound_id=row_id,
346	            original_content=content,
347	            suggested_rewrite=verdict.get("suggested_rewrite"),
348	            reason=verdict["reason"],
349	            verdict="block",
350	            checker_failed=bool(verdict.get("checker_failed")),
351	        )
352	        return row_id
353	    if verdict["verdict"] == "rewrite":
354	        await _append_turn_reasoning(pool, bot_turn_id, f"Outbound withheld for OOB rewrite review: {verdict['reason']}")
355	        row_id = await _insert_outbound(pool, user, content, "withheld", bot_turn_id=bot_turn_id)
356	        await record_withheld_outbound_review(
357	            pool,
358	            recipient_id=user.id,
359	            outbound_id=row_id,
360	            original_content=content,
361	            suggested_rewrite=verdict.get("suggested_rewrite"),
362	            reason=verdict["reason"],
363	            verdict="rewrite",
364	            checker_failed=bool(verdict.get("checker_failed")),
365	        )
366	        return row_id
367	    if verdict.get("checker_failed"):
368	        logger.warning("OOB checker failed open for recipient_id=%s: %s", user.id, verdict.get("reason"))
369	
370	    provider = get_settings().messaging_provider.strip().lower()
371	    if provider == "discord":
372	        within_window = True
373	    else:
374	        last_inbound_at = await pool.fetchval(
375	            "SELECT MAX(sent_at) FROM messages WHERE sender_id=$1 AND direction='inbound'",
376	            user.id,
377	        )
378	        within_window = last_inbound_at is not None and datetime.now(UTC) - last_inbound_at < timedelta(hours=24)
379	
380	    if not within_window and template_fallback is None:
381	        await _append_turn_reasoning(pool, bot_turn_id, "Outbound deferred: outside WhatsApp 24h window with no template")
382	        return await _insert_outbound(pool, user, content, "withheld", bot_turn_id=bot_turn_id)
383	
384	    template_payload = None
385	    if not within_window:
386	        template_payload = render_template(template_fallback)
387	
388	    if before_provider_send is not None:
389	        await before_provider_send()
390	
391	    row_id = await _insert_outbound(pool, user, content, bot_turn_id=bot_turn_id)
392	
393	    async def send_call() -> dict[str, Any]:
394	        if provider == "discord":
395	            return await discord.send_text(user.phone, content, send_typing_indicator=send_typing_indicator)
396	        if within_window:
397	            return await whatsapp.send_text(user.phone, content)
398	        return await whatsapp.send_template(user.phone, template_payload)
399	
400	    try:
401	        response = await _send_with_retry(send_call)
402	    except Exception as exc:
403	        logger.warning("outbound send failed after retries: %s", exc)
404	        await pool.execute("UPDATE messages SET processing_state='expired' WHERE id=$1", row_id)
405	        await _append_turn_reasoning(pool, bot_turn_id, f"Outbound send failed: {exc}")
406	        return row_id
407	
408	    wa_id = response["messages"][0]["id"]
409	    await pool.execute(
410	        "UPDATE messages SET whatsapp_message_id=$1, processing_state='processed' WHERE id=$2",
411	        wa_id,
412	        row_id,
413	    )
414	    await claim_onboarding_welcome(pool, user.id)
415	    return row_id
416

1	"""Per-user inbound burst coalescing."""
2	
3	import asyncio
4	from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
5	from dataclasses import dataclass
6	from typing import Any
7	from uuid import UUID
8	
9	from resident_chat_runtime.coalescing import AsyncBurstCoalescer, BurstBatch
10	
11	from app.models.user import User
12	from app.services.pacer import PacingDecision
13	
14	
15	@dataclass
16	class _Burst:
17	    message_ids: list[UUID]
18	    user: User
19	    first_seen_at: float
20	    source: str = "live"
21	
22	
23	class BurstCoalescer:
24	    def __init__(
25	        self,
26	        on_burst_complete: Callable[[list[UUID], User], Awaitable[None]],
27	        *,
28	        debounce_seconds: float = 10.0,
29	        max_seconds: float = 30.0,
30	        pacer: Any | None = None,
31	        on_paced_answer: Callable[[list[UUID], User, PacingDecision], Awaitable[None]] | None = None,
32	        on_paced_reaction: Callable[[list[UUID], User, PacingDecision], Awaitable[None]] | None = None,
33	        on_live_typing: Callable[[User, asyncio.Event], Awaitable[None]] | None = None,
34	    ) -> None:
35	        self.on_burst_complete = on_burst_complete
36	        self.on_paced_answer = on_paced_answer
37	        self.on_paced_reaction = on_paced_reaction
38	        self.on_live_typing = on_live_typing
39	        self.pacer = pacer
40	        self.debounce_seconds = debounce_seconds
41	        self.max_seconds = max_seconds
42	        self._bursts: dict[UUID, _Burst] = {}
43	        self._locks: dict[UUID, asyncio.Lock] = {}
44	        self._wait_tasks: dict[UUID, asyncio.Task] = {}
45	        self._live_typing_tasks: dict[UUID, asyncio.Task[None]] = {}
46	        self._live_typing_stops: dict[UUID, asyncio.Event] = {}
47	        self._coalescer: AsyncBurstCoalescer[UUID, UUID] = AsyncBurstCoalescer(
48	            self._fire_batch,
49	            idle_delay=debounce_seconds,
50	            max_delay=max_seconds,
51	        )
52	
53	    async def add(self, user_id: UUID, message_id: UUID, user: User, *, source: str = "live") -> None:
54	        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
55	        lock = self._locks.setdefault(user_id, asyncio.Lock())
56	        async with lock:
57	            wait_task = self._wait_tasks.pop(user_id, None)
58	            if wait_task is not None:
59	                wait_task.cancel()
60	            burst = self._bursts.get(user_id)
61	            if burst is None:
62	                burst = _Burst(message_ids=[], user=user, first_seen_at=loop.time(), source=source)
63	                self._bursts[user_id] = burst
64	            burst.message_ids.append(message_id)
65	            burst.user = user
66	            burst.source = self._merge_source(burst.source, source)
67	            if source == "live" and self.on_live_typing is not None and user_id not in self._live_typing_tasks:
68	                stop_event = asyncio.Event()
69	                self._live_typing_stops[user_id] = stop_event
70	                self._live_typing_tasks[user_id] = asyncio.create_task(self.on_live_typing(user, stop_event))
71	            await self._coalescer.submit(user_id, message_id)
72	
73	    async def add_burst(self, user_id: UUID, message_ids: list[UUID], user: User) -> None:
74	        await self.on_burst_complete(message_ids, user)
75	
76	    async def _fire(self, user_id: UUID) -> None:
77	        await self._coalescer.flush(user_id)
78	
79	    async def _fire_batch(self, batch: BurstBatch[UUID, UUID]) -> None:
80	        lock = self._locks.setdefault(batch.key, asyncio.Lock())
81	        async with lock:
82	            burst = self._bursts.pop(batch.key, None)
83	            if burst is None:
84	                return
85	            await self._handle_ready_burst(batch.key, burst)
86	
87	    def snapshot(self) -> dict[UUID, Any]:
88	        return self._bursts
89	
90	    async def _handle_ready_burst(self, user_id: UUID, burst: _Burst) -> None:
91	        message_ids = list(burst.message_ids)
92	        await self._stop_live_typing(user_id)
93	        if self.pacer is None:
94	            # Runtime contract: on_burst_complete(triggering_message_ids: list[UUID], user).
95	            await self.on_burst_complete(message_ids, burst.user)
96	            return
97	
98	        decision = await self.pacer.decide_and_record(burst.user, message_ids, source=burst.source)
99	        if decision.action == "wait":
100	            self._bursts[user_id] = burst
101	            self._wait_tasks[user_id] = asyncio.create_task(self._fire_after_wait(user_id, max(0.0, decision.wait_s)))
102	            return
103	        if decision.action == "answer":
104	            await self._call_paced_answer(message_ids, burst.user, decision)
105	            return
106	        if decision.action == "react":
107	            if self.on_paced_reaction is not None:
108	                await self.on_paced_reaction(message_ids, burst.user, decision)
109	            await self._mark_processed(message_ids)
110	            return
111	        if decision.action == "silence":
112	            await self._mark_processed(message_ids)
113	            return
114	        await self._call_paced_answer(message_ids, burst.user, decision)
115	
116	    async def _stop_live_typing(self, user_id: UUID) -> None:
117	        stop_event = self._live_typing_stops.pop(user_id, None)
118	        task = self._live_typing_tasks.pop(user_id, None)
119	        if stop_event is not None:
120	            stop_event.set()
121	        if task is not None:
122	            try:
123	                await task
124	            except Exception:
125	                pass
126	
127	    async def _fire_after_wait(self, user_id: UUID, wait_s: float) -> None:
128	        try:
129	            await asyncio.sleep(wait_s)
130	            lock = self._locks.setdefault(user_id, asyncio.Lock())
131	            async with lock:
132	                self._wait_tasks.pop(user_id, None)
133	                burst = self._bursts.pop(user_id, None)
134	                if burst is not None:
135	                    await self._handle_ready_burst(user_id, burst)
136	        except asyncio.CancelledError:
137	            raise
138	
139	    async def _call_paced_answer(self, message_ids: list[UUID], user: User, decision: PacingDecision) -> None:
140	        if self.on_paced_answer is not None:
141	            await self.on_paced_answer(message_ids, user, decision)
142	            return
143	        await self.on_burst_complete(message_ids, user)
144	
145	    async def _mark_processed(self, message_ids: list[UUID]) -> None:
146	        pool = getattr(self.pacer, "pool", None)
147	        if pool is None or not message_ids:
148	            return
149	        await pool.execute(
150	            "UPDATE messages SET processing_state='processed' WHERE id = ANY($1::uuid[]) AND processing_state='raw'",
151	            message_ids,
152	        )
153	
154	    def _merge_source(self, existing: str, incoming: str) -> str:
155	        if existing == incoming:
156	            return existing
157	        priority = {
158	            "recovery": 4,
159	            "catch_up": 4,
160	            "media": 3,
161	            "mixed": 2,
162	            "live": 1,
163	        }
164	        existing_priority = priority.get(existing, 2)
165	        incoming_priority = priority.get(incoming, 2)
166	        if existing_priority > incoming_priority:
167	            return existing
168	        if incoming_priority > existing_priority:
169	            return incoming
170	        if existing in {"recovery", "catch_up"}:
171	            return existing
172	        if incoming in {"recovery", "catch_up"}:
173	            return incoming
174	        return "mixed"
175

216	async def _record_response_cost(pool: Any, usage: Any) -> None:
217	    settings = get_settings()
218	    input_price = settings.anthropic_input_usd_per_mtok
219	    output_price = settings.anthropic_output_usd_per_mtok
220	    input_tokens = _usage_tokens(usage, "input_tokens")
221	    cache_create = _usage_tokens(usage, "cache_creation_input_tokens")
222	    cache_read = _usage_tokens(usage, "cache_read_input_tokens")
223	    output_tokens = _usage_tokens(usage, "output_tokens")
224	    regular_input_tokens = max(0, input_tokens - cache_create - cache_read)
225	    dollars = (
226	        regular_input_tokens * input_price
227	        + cache_create * input_price * 1.25
228	        + cache_read * input_price * 0.10
229	        + output_tokens * output_price
230	    ) / 1_000_000
231	    await record_llm_cost(pool, "text", dollars)
232	
233	
234	async def _create_message_with_retry(
235	    client: Any,
236	    *,
237	    ctx: TurnContext,
238	    system: list[dict[str, Any]],
239	    tools: list[dict[str, Any]],
240	    messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
241	    model: str | None = None,
242	    max_tokens: int = 1200,
243	) -> Any:
244	    settings = get_settings()
245	    last_error: Exception | None = None
246	    for attempt in range(2):
247	        if not await is_under_cap(ctx.pool, "text"):
248	            raise SpendCapExceeded("text LLM spend cap exceeded")
249	        try:
250	            response = await client.messages.create(
251	                model=model or settings.conversational_model,
252	                max_tokens=max_tokens,
253	                system=system,
254	                messages=messages,
255	                tools=tools,
256	            )
257	        except Exception as exc:  # Anthropic SDK transient subclasses vary by version.
258	            last_error = exc
259	            if attempt == 0:
260	                logger.warning("anthropic message create failed; retrying once: %s", exc)
261	                continue
262	            raise LLMPhaseError(str(exc)) from exc
263	        await _record_response_cost(ctx.pool, _attr(response, "usage", {}))
264	        return response
265	    raise LLMPhaseError(str(last_error or "anthropic message create failed"))
266

268	async def run_step(
269	    client: Any,
270	    ctx: TurnContext,
271	    system_prompt: str,
272	    hot_context_rendered: str,
273	    allowed_tools: set[str],
274	    seed_messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
275	    model: str | None = None,
276	    max_tokens: int = 1200,
277	    max_tool_iterations: int | None = None,
278	) -> tuple[str, list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
279	    settings = get_settings()
280	    if client is None:
281	        client = anthropic.AsyncAnthropic([REDACTED]())
282	
283	    system = _system_blocks(system_prompt, hot_context_rendered)
284	    tools = _anthropic_tools(allowed_tools)
285	    messages = list(seed_messages)
286	    tool_call_count = 0
287	    tool_iteration_count = 0
288	
289	    while True:
290	        response = await _create_message_with_retry(
291	            client,
292	            ctx=ctx,
293	            system=system,
294	            tools=tools,
295	            messages=messages,
296	            model=model,
297	            max_tokens=max_tokens,
298	        )
299	        content_blocks = [_block_to_dict(block) for block in (_attr(response, "content", []) or [])]
300	        messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": content_blocks})
301	        tool_uses = [block for block in content_blocks if block.get("type") == "tool_use"]
302	        if not tool_uses or _attr(response, "stop_reason") != "tool_use":
303	            final_text = "\n".join(
304	                str(block.get("text", "")).strip()
305	                for block in content_blocks
306	                if block.get("type") == "text" and str(block.get("text", "")).strip()
307	            )
308	            return final_text, messages, tool_call_count
309	
310	        tool_iteration_count += 1
311	        if max_tool_iterations is not None and tool_iteration_count > max_tool_iterations:
312	            raise BoundedLoopExceeded(f"tool iteration cap exceeded: {max_tool_iterations}")
313	        tool_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
314	        for tool_use in tool_uses:
315	            if tool_use["name"] != "update_turn_plan":
316	                tool_call_count += 1
317	            result = await call_tool(tool_use["name"], tool_use.get("input") or {}, ctx)
318	            is_error = bool(result.get("is_error") or result.get("error"))
319	            tool_results.append(
320	                {
321	                    "type": "tool_result",
322	                    "tool_use_id": tool_use["id"],
323	                    "content": json.dumps(result, default=str),
324	                    "is_error": is_error,
325	                }
326	            )
327	        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": tool_results})

344	def _explicit_partner_alert_requested(hot_context: Any) -> bool:
345	    if bool(hot_context.trigger_metadata.get("explicit_partner_alert_requested")):
346	        return True
347	    messages = hot_context.trigger_metadata.get("messages", [])
348	    for message in messages:
349	        content = str(message.get("content") or "").lower()
350	        if not content:
351	            continue
352	        asks_to_alert = any(phrase in content for phrase in ("tell", "alert", "let", "message", "ask"))
353	        names_partner = any(phrase in content for phrase in ("partner", "him", "her", "them"))
354	        if asks_to_alert and names_partner:
355	            return True
356	    return False

359	def _collect_reasoning(messages: list[dict[str, Any]], final_text: str = "") -> str:
360	    fragments: list[str] = []
361	    for message in messages:
362	        if message.get("role") != "assistant":
363	            continue
364	        content = message.get("content", "")
365	        blocks = content if isinstance(content, list) else [{"type": "text", "text": content}]
366	        for block in blocks:
367	            if isinstance(block, dict) and block.get("type") == "text":
368	                text = str(block.get("text", "")).strip()
369	                if text and text != final_text:
370	                    fragments.append(text)
371	    return "\n".join(fragments)

387	def _extract_reaction_directive(text: str) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
388	    emoji: str | None = None
389	    kept_lines: list[str] = []
390	    for raw_line in text.splitlines():
391	        match = REACTION_DIRECTIVE_RE.match(raw_line)
392	        if match and emoji is None:
393	            emoji = match.group("emoji").strip()
394	            continue

396	    return emoji, "\n".join(kept_lines).strip()

400	    settings = get_settings()
401	    if settings.messaging_provider.strip().lower() != "discord" or not triggering_message_ids:

418	async def _check_outbound_oob(
419	    pool: Any,
420	    content: str,
421	    recipient_id: UUID,
422	    protected_owner_ids: list[UUID] | None = None,
423	) -> dict[str, Any]:
424	    hook = hooks.check_oob
425	    if hook is None:
426	        return {"verdict": "ok", "reason": "OOB hook disabled", "suggested_rewrite": None, "checker_failed": False}
427	    try:
428	        verdict = await hook(pool, content, recipient_id, protected_owner_ids=protected_owner_ids)
429	    except TypeError:
430	        try:
431	            verdict = await hook(pool, content, recipient_id)
432	        except TypeError:
433	            verdict = await hook(content, recipient_id)
434	    if hasattr(verdict, "model_dump"):
435	        verdict = verdict.model_dump(mode="json")
436	    verdict.setdefault("suggested_rewrite", verdict.get("rewrite"))
437	    verdict.setdefault("reason", "")
438	    verdict.setdefault("checker_failed", False)
439	    return verdict

1	BEGIN;
2	
3	-- ============================================================
4	-- Sprint 1: Foundation schema — topics, bots, bindings, channels, identities
5	-- ============================================================
6	
7	-- 1. Topics — the central organizing concept for all artifacts
8	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS topics (
9	    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
10	    slug text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
11	    display_name text NOT NULL,
12	    description text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
13	    participants_shape text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'dyad' CHECK (participants_shape IN ('dyad', 'solo', 'group')),
14	    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
15	);
16	
17	-- 2. Bots — intentionally thin; scope/version fields live in code per §3
18	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bots (
19	    id text PRIMARY KEY,
20	    display_name text NOT NULL,
21	    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
22	);
23	
24	-- 3. Dyads — a single row per relationship pair
25	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dyads (
26	    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
27	    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
28	);
29	
30	-- 4. Dyad members — links users to their dyad
31	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dyad_members (
32	    dyad_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES dyads(id),
33	    user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
34	    joined_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
35	    PRIMARY KEY (dyad_id, user_id)
36	);
37	
38	-- 5. Bot bindings — which bots are bound to which dyads/users
39	-- XOR: exactly one of dyad_id or user_id must be non-null
40	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bot_bindings (
41	    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
42	    bot_id text NOT NULL REFERENCES bots(id),
43	    dyad_id uuid REFERENCES dyads(id),
44	    user_id uuid REFERENCES users(id),
45	    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
46	    CONSTRAINT bot_bindings_xor CHECK ((user_id IS NOT NULL) <> (dyad_id IS NOT NULL))
47	);
48	
49	-- 6. Channels — transport-specific addresses where bots listen
50	-- Two uniqueness mechanisms:
51	--   (a) raw-column UNIQUE for standard direct-match lookups
52	--   (b) expression index for COALESCE'd ON CONFLICT used by seed_channels.py
53	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS channels (
54	    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
55	    bot_id text NOT NULL REFERENCES bots(id),
56	    transport text NOT NULL CHECK (transport IN ('discord', 'whatsapp', 'sms', 'web')),
57	    address text NOT NULL,
58	    guild_id text,
59	    channel_id text,
60	    config jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
61	    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
62	    UNIQUE (transport, address, guild_id, channel_id)
63	);
64	
65	-- Expression-based unique index: COALESCE treats NULL guild_id/channel_id as empty string,
66	-- allowing seed_channels.py to use ON CONFLICT (transport, address, COALESCE(guild_id,''), COALESCE(channel_id,''))
67	-- Without this index, the expression-based ON CONFLICT target would fail with
68	-- 'no unique or exclusion constraint matching the on conflict specification'.
69	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS channels_uniq_coalesced
70	    ON channels (transport, address, COALESCE(guild_id, ''), COALESCE(channel_id, ''));
71	
72	-- 7. User identities — maps transport+address pairs to user records
73	-- verified_at is nullable; account-linking workflows fill it in S4+
74	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_identities (
75	    transport text NOT NULL CHECK (transport IN ('discord', 'whatsapp', 'sms', 'legacy')),
76	    address text NOT NULL,
77	    user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
78	    verified_at timestamptz,
79	    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
80	    PRIMARY KEY (transport, address)
81	);
82	
83	-- ============================================================
84	-- Seed data
85	-- ============================================================
86	
87	-- Seed the 'relationship' topic — the default scope for the mediator
88	INSERT INTO topics (slug, display_name, description, participants_shape)
89	VALUES ('relationship', 'Relationship', 'Default relationship mediation topic', 'dyad')
90	ON CONFLICT (slug) DO NOTHING;
91	
92	-- Seed the mediator bot
93	INSERT INTO bots (id, display_name)
94	VALUES ('mediator', 'Mediator')
95	ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;
96	
97	-- Seed a single dyad and link it to the existing users.
98	-- Uses an existence-check DO block rather than ON CONFLICT on a random PK
99	-- so that re-running the migration is a true no-op.
100	DO $$
101	DECLARE
102	    _dyad_id uuid;
103	    _user_a_id uuid;
104	    _user_b_id uuid;
105	BEGIN
106	    -- Check if any dyad already has a binding for mediator
107	    PERFORM 1 FROM bot_bindings WHERE bot_id = 'mediator' AND dyad_id IS NOT NULL;
108	    IF FOUND THEN
109	        RETURN;
110	    END IF;
111	
112	    -- Find two users to form the dyad
113	    SELECT id INTO _user_a_id FROM users ORDER BY created_at LIMIT 1;
114	    SELECT id INTO _user_b_id FROM users WHERE id <> _user_a_id ORDER BY created_at LIMIT 1;
115	
116	    IF _user_a_id IS NULL OR _user_b_id IS NULL THEN
117	        -- Not enough users — skip dyad creation; seed_channels.sql populates identities
118	        -- and dyad can be formed later
119	        RETURN;
120	    END IF;
121	
122	    -- Create dyad
123	    INSERT INTO dyads DEFAULT VALUES RETURNING id INTO _dyad_id;
124	
125	    -- Add members
126	    INSERT INTO dyad_members (dyad_id, user_id) VALUES (_dyad_id, _user_a_id);
127	    INSERT INTO dyad_members (dyad_id, user_id) VALUES (_dyad_id, _user_b_id);
128	
129	    -- Create bot binding for mediator to this dyad
130	    INSERT INTO bot_bindings (bot_id, dyad_id) VALUES ('mediator', _dyad_id);
131	END $$;
132	
133	-- ============================================================
134	-- Backfill user_identities from users.phone
135	-- transport='legacy' per locked decision §16.8 (all existing rows).
136	-- ============================================================
137	INSERT INTO user_identities (transport, address, user_id)
138	SELECT 'legacy', phone, id
139	FROM users
140	WHERE phone IS NOT NULL
141	ON CONFLICT (transport, address) DO NOTHING;
142	
143	-- ============================================================
144	-- Indexes
145	-- ============================================================
146	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bot_bindings_bot_dyad ON bot_bindings (bot_id, dyad_id) WHERE dyad_id IS NOT NULL;
147	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bot_bindings_bot_user ON bot_bindings (bot_id, user_id) WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL;
148	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_channels_bot_transport ON channels (bot_id, transport);
149	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_identities_user ON user_identities (user_id);
150	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_dyad_members_user ON dyad_members (user_id);
151	
152	-- ============================================================
153	-- RLS: deny anon on all new tables
154	-- ============================================================
155	
156	ALTER TABLE topics ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
157	ALTER TABLE topics FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
158	REVOKE ALL ON TABLE topics FROM anon;
159	
160	ALTER TABLE bots ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
161	ALTER TABLE bots FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
162	REVOKE ALL ON TABLE bots FROM anon;
163	
164	ALTER TABLE dyads ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
165	ALTER TABLE dyads FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
166	REVOKE ALL ON TABLE dyads FROM anon;
167	
168	ALTER TABLE dyad_members ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
169	ALTER TABLE dyad_members FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
170	REVOKE ALL ON TABLE dyad_members FROM anon;
171	
172	ALTER TABLE bot_bindings ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
173	ALTER TABLE bot_bindings FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
174	REVOKE ALL ON TABLE bot_bindings FROM anon;
175	
176	ALTER TABLE channels ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
177	ALTER TABLE channels FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
178	REVOKE ALL ON TABLE channels FROM anon;
179	
180	ALTER TABLE user_identities ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
181	ALTER TABLE user_identities FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
182	REVOKE ALL ON TABLE user_identities FROM anon;
183	
184	-- Deny policies (idempotent via DO block with duplicate_object catch)
185	DO $$
186	BEGIN
187	    CREATE POLICY deny_anon_topics ON topics FOR ALL TO anon USING (false) WITH CHECK (false);
188	EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
189	END $$;

1	BEGIN;
2	
3	-- ============================================================
4	-- Sprint 1: artifact_topics — polymorphic join table linking artifacts to topics
5	-- ============================================================
6	
7	-- Polymorphic join: (artifact_table, artifact_id) identifies the artifact row.
8	-- No FK to individual artifact tables — orphan prevention is application
9	-- discipline; lint catches it in S2a.
10	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifact_topics (
11	    artifact_table text NOT NULL,
12	    artifact_id uuid NOT NULL,
13	    topic_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES topics(id),
14	    status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (status IN ('active', 'retired')),
15	    tagged_by_bot_id text REFERENCES bots(id),
16	    reason text,
17	    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
18	    retired_at timestamptz,
19	    PRIMARY KEY (artifact_table, artifact_id, topic_id)
20	);
21	
22	-- ============================================================
23	-- Partial unique indexes — enforce at most one active row per (artifact, topic).
24	-- Retired rows coexist freely (historical record).
25	-- ============================================================
26	
27	-- Prevent duplicate active assignments for the same (artifact_table, artifact_id)
28	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS artifact_topics_active_artifact_key
29	    ON artifact_topics (artifact_table, artifact_id)
30	    WHERE status = 'active';
31	
32	-- Prevent multiple active rows for the same (artifact_table, artifact_id, topic_id)
33	-- (covers the case where the same artifact is assigned to the same topic twice)
34	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS artifact_topics_active_unique_key
35	    ON artifact_topics (artifact_table, artifact_id, topic_id)
36	    WHERE status = 'active';
37	
38	-- ============================================================
39	-- Indexes
40	-- ============================================================
41	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_artifact_topics_topic_status
42	    ON artifact_topics (topic_id, status);
43	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_artifact_topics_tagged_by
44	    ON artifact_topics (tagged_by_bot_id) WHERE tagged_by_bot_id IS NOT NULL;
45	
46	-- ============================================================
47	-- RLS: deny anon
48	-- ============================================================
49	ALTER TABLE artifact_topics ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
50	ALTER TABLE artifact_topics FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
51	REVOKE ALL ON TABLE artifact_topics FROM anon;
52	
53	DO $$
54	BEGIN
55	    CREATE POLICY deny_anon_artifact_topics ON artifact_topics
56	        FOR ALL TO anon USING (false) WITH CHECK (false);
57	EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
58	END $$;
59	
60	COMMIT;
```

8	-- 1. topic_status — each row captures a bot-authored status update scoped to

10	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS topic_status (
11	    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
12	    topic_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES topics(id),
13	    dyad_id uuid REFERENCES dyads(id),
14	    user_id uuid REFERENCES users(id),

17	    last_updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
18	    last_updated_by_bot_id text NOT NULL REFERENCES bots(id),
19	    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
20	    CONSTRAINT topic_status_xor CHECK ((user_id IS NOT NULL) <> (dyad_id IS NOT NULL))
21	);
22	
23	-- Partial unique indexes: at most one status row per (topic, user) or (topic, dyad)

133	def _emoji_score(query_terms: set[str], name: str, aliases: list[str], keywords: list[str]) -> int:
134	    haystacks = [name, *aliases, *keywords]
135	    score = 0
136	    for term in query_terms:
137	        for idx, haystack in enumerate(haystacks):
138	            normalized = haystack.lower().replace("_", " ").replace("-", " ")
139	            if term == normalized:
140	                score += 12 if idx == 0 else 8
141	            elif term in _emoji_terms(normalized):
142	                score += 6 if idx == 0 else 4
143	            elif term in normalized:
144	                score += 2
145	    return score
146

148	class NewerInboundDuringPacedSend(Exception):
149	    pass

152	async def _newer_inbound_exists(ctx: TurnContext) -> bool:
153	    boundary = ctx.turn_started_at
154	    if ctx.triggering_message_ids:
155	        trigger_boundary = await ctx.pool.fetchval(
156	            "SELECT MAX(sent_at) FROM messages WHERE id = ANY($1::uuid[])",
157	            ctx.triggering_message_ids,
158	        )
159	        if trigger_boundary is not None:
160	            boundary = trigger_boundary
161	    if boundary is None:
162	        return False
163	    return bool(
164	        await ctx.pool.fetchval(
165	            """
166	            SELECT EXISTS (
167	                SELECT 1
168	                FROM messages
169	                WHERE direction='inbound'
170	                  AND sender_id=$1
171	                  AND sent_at > $2
172	                  AND NOT (id = ANY($3::uuid[]))
173	            )
174	            """,
175	            ctx.user.id,
176	            boundary,
177	            ctx.triggering_message_ids,
178	        )
179	    )

189	    if (
190	        not ctx.incremental_sending_enabled
191	        or settings.messaging_provider.strip().lower() != "discord"
192	        or not settings.discord_multi_message_enabled
193	    ):
194	        return SendMessagePartOutput(

25	    except KeyError as exc:
26	        known = ", ".join(sorted(BOT_SPECS))

29	
30	async def populate_mediator_spec_from_db(pool: Any) -> None:
31	    """Read mediator display_name from the bots table and rebuild MEDIATOR_BOT.
32	
33	    All scope/topic/version fields are hardcoded mediator defaults.
34	    Only display_name comes from the database — the bots table is intentionally
35	    thin per §3. On miss, warns and keeps code defaults unchanged.
36	    """
37	    try:
38	        row = await pool.fetchrow(
39	            "SELECT display_name FROM bots WHERE id = 'mediator'"
40	        )
41	    except Exception:
42	        logger.warning(
43	            "populate_mediator_spec_from_db: could not query bots table — "
44	            "keeping module-level defaults",
45	            exc_info=True,
46	        )
47	        return
48	    if row is None:
49	        logger.warning(
50	            "populate_mediator_spec_from_db: no mediator row in bots table — "
51	            "keeping module-level defaults"
52	        )
53	        return
54	
55	    display_name = row["display_name"]
56	    # Reconstruct MediatorBotSpec with DB display_name + hardcoded defaults
57	    from app.bots.mediator import MediatorBotSpec, MEDIATOR_STEP_INSTRUCTIONS
58	
59	    rebuilt = MediatorBotSpec(
60	        bot_id="mediator",
61	        prompt_renderer=MEDIATOR_BOT.prompt_renderer,
62	        step_instructions=MEDIATOR_STEP_INSTRUCTIONS,
63	        skeleton_overrides=MEDIATOR_BOT.skeleton_overrides,
64	        display_name=display_name,
65	        primary_topic_slug="relationship",
66	        participants_shape="dyad",
67	        read_scopes=ReadScopes(),
68	        write_scopes=WriteScopes(),
69	        # Preserve version fields so anything the in-code MEDIATOR_BOT
70	        # already had set survives the rebuild.
71	        bot_spec_version=MEDIATOR_BOT.bot_spec_version,
72	        hot_context_builder_version=MEDIATOR_BOT.hot_context_builder_version,
73	        tool_schema_version=MEDIATOR_BOT.tool_schema_version,
74	    )
75	    BOT_SPECS["mediator"] = rebuilt
76	    logger.info("populate_mediator_spec_from_db: mediator spec updated (display_name=%s)", display_name)
77

76	        charge: str | None,

80	        pacing_context = trigger_metadata.get("pacing")
81	        pacing_seed = (
82	            f" pacing={json.dumps(pacing_context, default=str)}."
83	            if pacing_context is not None
84	            else ""
85	        )
86	        return [
87	            {
88	                "role": "user",
89	                "content": (
90	                    f"Trigger: kind={trigger_metadata.get('kind', 'inbound')} "
91	                    f"ids={triggering_message_ids} charge={charge or 'routine'} "
92	                    f"context={trigger_metadata.get('context', {})}."
93	                    f"{pacing_seed} "
94	                    f"{orient_header}\n\n"
95	                    f"Turn plan:\n{plan.render_checklist()}\n\n"
96	                    f"Current step instruction: {self.step_instructions[plan.current]}"
97	                ),
98	            }
99	        ]
100	
101	    def build_step_transition_message(
102	        self,
103	        *,
104	        plan: TurnPlan,
105	        sent_summary: str | None = None,
106	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
107	        parts = [
108	            f"Next step: {plan.current}.",
109	            "Current task: execute only the highlighted step. If nothing is needed, return an empty assistant response with no tool calls.",
110	            f"Turn plan:\n{plan.render_checklist()}",
111	        ]
112	        if sent_summary is not None and plan.current in {"record", "schedule"}:
113	            parts.append(sent_summary)
114	        parts.append(f"Current step instruction: {self.step_instructions[plan.current]}")
115	        return {
116	            "role": "user",
117	            "content": "\n\n".join(parts),
118	        }

27	
28	async def pause(pool: Any, paused_by_user_id: UUID, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> None:
29	    now = now or _utc_now()
30	    await pool.execute(
31	        """
32	        INSERT INTO system_state (key, value, paused_at, paused_by_user_id, updated_at)

35	        SET paused_at = EXCLUDED.paused_at,
36	            paused_by_user_id = EXCLUDED.paused_by_user_id,
37	            updated_at = EXCLUDED.updated_at
38	        """,
39	        now,
40	        paused_by_user_id,
41	    )
42	
43	
44	async def resume(pool: Any, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> None:

47	        """
48	        INSERT INTO system_state (key, value, paused_at, paused_by_user_id, updated_at)
49	        VALUES ('global_pause', '{}'::jsonb, NULL, NULL, $1)

51	        SET paused_at = NULL,
52	            paused_by_user_id = NULL,
53	            updated_at = EXCLUDED.updated_at

23	
24	    run = subparsers.add_parser("run", help="Run scripted eval scenarios.")
25	    run.add_argument("--scenario", help="Run only the named scenario.")
26	    run.add_argument("--tag", help="Run scenarios with this tag.")
27	    run.add_argument("--prompt-version", default="v1", help="Prompt version to evaluate.")
28	    run.add_argument("--cost-cap-usd", default="5", help="Maximum eval cost for the run.")
29	    run.add_argument("--database-url", help="Non-production eval database URL. Defaults to EVAL_DATABASE_URL/DATABASE_URL.")
30	    run.add_argument("--keep-db", action="store_true", help="Keep the scratch schema after the run for debugging.")
31	    run.add_argument(
32	        "--scenarios-dir",
33	        type=Path,
34	        default=Path(__file__).with_name("scenarios"),
35	        help="Directory containing markdown scenario files.",
36	    )
37	    run.set_defaults(func=_run)
38	
39	    replay = subparsers.add_parser("replay", help="Replay historical messages in eval mode.")
40	    replay.add_argument("--since", required=True, help="Inclusive start date or timestamp.")
41	    replay.add_argument("--user", required=True, help="User id to replay.")
42	    replay.add_argument("--prompt-version", default="v1", help="Prompt version to evaluate.")
43	    replay.add_argument("--database-url", help="Non-production eval database URL. Defaults to EVAL_DATABASE_URL/DATABASE_URL.")
44	    replay.add_argument("--keep-db", action="store_true", help="Keep the scratch schema after replay for debugging.")
45	    replay.set_defaults(func=_replay)
46	
47	    return parser
48	
49	
50	def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
51	    parser = build_parser()
52	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
53	    try:
54	        return args.func(args)
55	    except ScenarioError as exc:
56	        parser.exit(2, f"evals: error: {exc}\n")
57	
58	
59	def _run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
60	    scenarios = load_scenarios(args.scenarios_dir, scenario_name=args.scenario, tag=args.tag)
61	    if not scenarios:
62	        print(f"Loaded 0 scenario(s) for prompt version {args.prompt_version}.")
63	        return 0
64	    return asyncio.run(_run_async(args, scenarios))
65	
66	
67	async def _run_async(args: argparse.Namespace, scenarios: list) -> int:
68	    async with eval_database(args.database_url, keep_db=args.keep_db) as scratch:
69	        run_id = await create_eval_run(scratch.pool, prompt_version=args.prompt_version)
70	        report = await run_scenarios(
71	            scratch.pool,
72	            scenarios,
73	            prompt_version=args.prompt_version,
74	            cost_cap_usd=args.cost_cap_usd,
75	            result_pool=scratch.pool,
76	            run_id=run_id,
77	        )
78	    print(format_report(report))
79	    return 0 if report.passed else 1
80	
81	
82	def _replay(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
83	    asyncio.run(_replay_async(args))
84	    return 0
85	
86	
87	async def _replay_async(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
88	    app = _App(_State())
89	    async with db_lifespan(app):
90	        async with eval_database(args.database_url, keep_db=args.keep_db) as scratch:
91	            await replay_history(
92	                app.state.pool,
93	                scratch.pool,
94	                since=args.since,
95	                user_id=args.user,
96	                prompt_version=args.prompt_version,
97	                output=sys.stdout,
98	            )
99	
100
```

I've drafted the implementation plan. Key highlights:

- **Phase 0** captures the eval baseline first (against pre-stamp S1 state), so we have the regression reference before any code change.
- **Phase 1** wires `routing.py` into `inbound.py` and threads scope through `TurnContext` and the `bot_turns` INSERT (where `bot_spec_version`/`hot_context_builder_version`/`tool_schema_version` get computed via `hashlib.sha1` per S1 lesson #4).
- **Phase 2** stamps every remaining INSERT site (messages, scheduled_jobs, feedback, artifacts). For artifacts I'm proposing **single-statement CTEs** that include the `artifact_topics` write — Postgres runs each statement atomically, so this satisfies the "same transaction" rule without needing to refactor every call site to `pool.acquire()` + `conn.transaction()`.
- **Phase 3** rekeys the coalescer + suppression with dual-key lookups (`(user_id)` and `(user_id, bot_id)`, plus `bot_id IS NULL` half on suppression to catch legacy rows) and adds the per-(user, bot) pause read path.
- **Phase 4** adds structured observability fields, the **advisory** lint script, and tests in new `tests/test_s2a_*.py` files plus a `tests/_scope_helpers.py` (the dirty `conftest.py` is untouched).

Four clarifying questions are flagged — most consequential: the eval-baseline shape (JSON-only vs DB-dump-per-scenario), and whether `messages.channel_id` should get its own column or stay observability-only. Eleven assumptions documented to let work proceed if the user doesn't answer immediately.

## Sprint 2a — §16.2 verbatim

**Goal.** Insert sites write the new columns; coalescer and newer-inbound suppression are rekeyed with a dual-key transition; per-bot observability comes online; pre-S3 eval baseline captured. Mediator behavior unchanged. **No NOT NULL.**

Checklist file local to the sprint.

**Work items, ordered.**

1. Wire `routing.py` into `inbound.py:23-180`. Populate `TurnContext` with real scope fields (still mediator-only defaults).
2. Stamp insert sites:
   - `inbound.py:161` — `bot_id`, `topic_id`, `channel_id`.
   - `messaging.py:55, 84` — same on outbound.
   - `agentic.py:483` — `bot_id`, `topic_id`, `bot_spec_version` (hash of `BotSpec` repr), `hot_context_builder_version`, `tool_schema_version` (hash of `tool_schemas.py`).
   - `agentic.py:577` deferred jobs and `scheduled_jobs.py:216` scheduler seeds — stamp `bot_id`, `topic_id`.
   - `write_tools.py:1367` scheduled-task writes — same.
   - Every artifact write in `write_tools.py`: stamp `recorded_by_bot_id` AND create matching `artifact_topics` row(s) for the bot's primary topic in the same transaction.

Bursts in-flight at deploy flush under old key; new bursts use the composite key. Drop the legacy reader in S2b.
4. **Dual-key newer-inbound suppression**:
   - `agentic.py:612` (final-send suppression).
   - `read_tools.py:166` (incremental-send suppression).

- Full pytest green, no regressions.
- Every new INSERT statement either includes `bot_id`/`topic_id` or is flagged by the lint (advisory).

## S1 lessons the executor must honor

1. **Before declaring any new script/CLI complete**: actually invoke it with `--help` (or equivalent entry point) to confirm it parses and basic argparse setup works. Pytest passing is necessary but not sufficient — pytest may bypass `main()`.
2. **Any SQL fragment in a Python string**: dry-run via `EXPLAIN` against the live schema, or sanity-check the aggregate/operator types against actual column types (e.g. `max(uuid)` doesn't exist in Postgres).
3. **Any asyncpg connection** against the configured `DATABASE_URL`: pass `statement_cache_size=0` — Supabase transaction-mode pooler (port 6543) is what's configured. Without this flag, scripts crash on first prepared-statement reuse.
4. **No `hash()` for deterministic IDs**: Python's `hash()` is salted per-process. Use `hashlib.sha1` for advisory-lock-id derivation or anything needing cross-run determinism.
5. **Schema filters in validation queries**: production tables live in schema `mediator`, NOT `public`. `information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = 'mediator'`.

## Stop conditions

- Any test that was passing on `main` starts failing — STOP, report.
- Any work item's executor decides the design is wrong and tries to redesign — STOP, report.
- Schema migration would touch existing rows destructively — STOP, report.
- Any artifact INSERT path can't fit `artifact_topics` write in the same transaction — STOP, report.

        Current plan (markdown):
        # Implementation Plan: S2a — Stamp insert sites, [REDACTED], observability, eval baseline

## Overview

S1 landed the additive schema (topics, bots, dyads, bindings, channels, identities, `artifact_topics`, `topic_status`, `user_bot_state`, nullable scope columns on `messages`/`bot_turns`/`scheduled_jobs`/`feedback`/`bridge_candidates`/artifact tables) and skeleton helpers (`routing.py`, extended `TurnContext` + `BotSpec`). S2a is the write-path pass:

- Wire `routing.py` into the inbound flow so the bot/sender/binding are resolved per inbound payload.
- Stamp every relevant INSERT with `bot_id`/`topic_id` (and version stamps on `bot_turns`), and add an `artifact_topics` row in the **same statement** as every artifact INSERT (single-statement CTE = same transaction).
- Rekey the in-memory `BurstCoalescer` and the newer-inbound suppression queries with dual-key `(user_id)` + `(user_id, bot_id)` lookups, with `bot_id IS NULL` accepted in suppression during transition.
- Read-path consult `user_bot_state.paused` in addition to `system_state` for the mediator binding (write path stays deferred).
- Pipe `bot_id`/`topic_id`/`channel_id`/`binding_id` into every relevant `logger`/`record_turn_event` call.
- Capture an eval baseline snapshot under `tests/fixtures/eval_baseline/` against the **pre-stamp** code (this runs day 1, before the stamping changes land — or alternatively against the un-stamped code on the previous commit; see assumption below).
- Add an **advisory** linter (`scripts/lint_inserts.py`) that warns on INSERTs missing `bot_id`/`topic_id` and on artifact INSERTs not co-statemented with `artifact_topics`. CI surfaces output without failing.

Hard guardrails: do not edit the 9 pre-existing dirty files (they predate this sprint), do not modify `docs/multi-agent-architecture.md`, add **no** `NOT NULL` constraints, do not redesign coalescer/suppression interfaces (dual-key, not full rename), and never let an artifact INSERT live without an accompanying `artifact_topics` row in the same statement — STOP and report if any site doesn't fit.

S1 lessons to honor explicitly: invoke any new CLI with `--help` before declaring done; use `hashlib.sha1` for any deterministic IDs / `bot_spec_version` (`hash()` is process-salted); set `statement_cache_size=0` on any new asyncpg connection; use schema `mediator` filters when querying `information_schema`; sanity-check SQL fragments against actual column types.

Repo shape relevant to S2a (file:line):
- Inbound: `app/services/inbound.py:147-244` (`_insert_message` + `process_inbound`).
- Outbound: `app/services/messaging.py:42-93` (`_insert_outbound`) plus calls at `:171`, `:198`, `:233`, `:336`, `:341`, `:355`, `:382`, `:391`.
- Agentic turn open: `app/services/agentic.py:473-502` (`_open_turn` INSERTs `bot_turns`).
- Deferred turn job: `app/services/agentic.py:569-590` (`_defer_for_text_cap` INSERTs `scheduled_jobs`).
- Newer-inbound suppression: `app/services/agentic.py:592-625` (final-send) and `app/services/tools/read_tools.py:152-179` (incremental-send).
- Scheduler seeds: `app/services/scheduled_jobs.py:205-227` (heartbeat) and `app/services/scheduled_job_handlers.py:198-215, 306-334` (recurring scheduled-task + weekly summary), `app/services/checkins.py:37`.
- Scheduled-task writes: `app/services/tools/write_tools.py:1359-1382` (`schedule_task`).
- Artifact writes: `app/services/tools/write_tools.py:695-757` (memories), `:760-793` (themes), `:796-848` (watch_items), `:868-915` (observations), `:918-1102` (distillations / `add_distillation` + `revise_distillation`), `:1105-1133` (out_of_bounds).
- Feedback INSERTs: `app/services/inbound.py:103-113`, `app/services/discord.py:470`, `app/services/tools/write_tools.py:1759-1775`.
- `_schedule_context_job` helper: `app/services/tools/write_tools.py:160-191` (used by watch_item_due, oob_review).
- Coalescer: `app/services/debouncer.py` (full file; dict keyed by `user_id`).
- Pause: `app/services/system_state.py` (full file).
- Routing: `app/services/routing.py` (full file — already exposes `resolve_bot`, `resolve_sender`, `resolve_binding`).
