4. **Stamp** `_schedule_context_job` (`write_tools.py:160-191`) — add `bot_id`/`topic_id` kwargs. Call sites (`add_watch_item`/`update_watch_item`, `add_oob`/`update_oob`) pass `ctx.bot_id`/`ctx.primary_topic_id`.
5. **Stamp** `schedule_task` (`write_tools.py:1367`) with `ctx.bot_id`/`ctx.primary_topic_id`.
6. **Stamp** the supersede-and-insert scheduled-job helper at `write_tools.py:181-191` — accept `bot_id`/`topic_id` kwargs.
7. **Scheduler dispatcher**: in the SELECT that claims due jobs (search `scheduled_job_handlers.py` for the FOR UPDATE / claim query), add `bot_id` and `topic_id` to the column list. **Handler signatures are unchanged** — every handler in `scheduled_job_handlers.py:78,98,112,134,145,149,160` already takes `job: dict[str, Any]` (grep-confirmed), so `bot_id`/`topic_id` arrive automatically as keys in `job`. No keyword surgery on individual handlers.
8. **Unit test** the dispatcher: instrumented handler asserts `job["bot_id"]` and `job["topic_id"]` are present (`tests/test_s2a_scheduler_dispatch_bot_id.py`).

### Step 8: Artifact writes + `artifact_topics` in same statement (`app/services/tools/write_tools.py`)
**Scope:** Large

> **Edit-safety contract for `write_tools.py`** (dirty file, INSERT-only carve-out):
> - Every change uses the `Edit` tool with a small unique `old_string` matching only the affected SQL fragment (column list, VALUES tuple, or one CTE arm).
> - **Do not** delete or re-flow surrounding code, comments, or whitespace.
> - **Each `write_tools.py` change lands in its own commit** so accidental reflow is caught at review time.
> - After every edit, run `git diff --stat app/services/tools/write_tools.py` and confirm only additive changes appear at the targeted line range.
> - For existing multi-arm CTEs (`supersede_memory`, `revise_distillation`), **ADD a new arm** — never rewrite.

1. **For each simple artifact INSERT** (memories `:699`, themes `:764`, watch_items `:800`, observations `:878`, distillations `:939`, out_of_bounds `:1109`), rewrite the SQL string from:
   ```sql
   INSERT INTO memories (...) VALUES (...) RETURNING id
   ```
   to:
   ```sql
   WITH new_artifact AS (
       INSERT INTO memories (..., recorded_by_bot_id) VALUES (..., $K) RETURNING id
   ), topic_link AS (
       INSERT INTO artifact_topics (artifact_table, artifact_id, topic_id, tagged_by_bot_id, status)
       SELECT 'memories', new_artifact.id, $M, $K, 'active' FROM new_artifact
   )
   SELECT id FROM new_artifact
   ```
   Postgres runs each statement atomically. `recorded_by_bot_id = ctx.bot_id`, `topic_id = ctx.primary_topic_id`.
2. **`supersede_memory` (`:737-757`)** — existing multi-arm CTE. **ADD** a third arm between the inner `INSERT INTO memories ... RETURNING` and the trailing `RETURNING`:
   ```sql
   , topic_link AS (
       INSERT INTO artifact_topics (artifact_table, artifact_id, topic_id, tagged_by_bot_id, status)
       SELECT 'memories', new.id, $M, $K, 'active' FROM new
   )
   ```
   Keep `WITH old AS (UPDATE memories ...)` verbatim.
3. **`revise_distillation` (`:1041-1092`)** — existing multi-arm CTE (`old`, `new`, `revised_old`). **ADD** a fourth arm:
   ```sql
   , topic_link AS (
       INSERT INTO artifact_topics (artifact_table, artifact_id, topic_id, tagged_by_bot_id, status)
       SELECT 'distillations', new.id, $M, $K, 'active' FROM new
   )
   ```
   Keep `revised_old` and the trailing `JOIN`/`SELECT` shape.
4. **STOP** and report if any artifact INSERT can't fit `artifact_topics` in the same statement. The `_schedule_context_job` follow-ups on `watch_items`/`out_of_bounds` are intentionally separate side-effects (artifact + topic link atomic; scheduled-job follow-up outside) and DO NOT trigger STOP.

### Step 9: Bridge candidate + feedback inserts (`app/services/tools/write_tools.py`, `app/services/discord.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Stamp** `bridge_candidates` INSERT at `write_tools.py:305-329`:
   - `bot_id = ctx.bot_id`
   - `topic_id = ctx.primary_topic_id`
   - `dyad_id = ctx.dyad_id` (NOT `ctx.binding_id` — they are distinct columns: `bot_bindings.id` vs `bot_bindings.dyad_id`). `ctx.dyad_id` is populated by `_resolve_scope` from `BindingResolution.dyad_id` per Step 4.1.
   Use the Step 8 edit-safety contract (targeted Edit on the column list + VALUES tuple only).
2. **Stamp** feedback INSERTs:
   - `write_tools.py:1763` (`log_feedback`): `bot_id=ctx.bot_id`, `topic_id=ctx.primary_topic_id`.
   - `discord.py:468-478` reaction handler: invoke `_resolve_scope` with the **bot's** address (matches `channels.address` per `scripts/seed_channels.py:95-101`):
     ```python
     bot_user_id = get_settings().discord_bot_user_id  # same env var the S1 seeder reads
     scope = await _resolve_scope(self.pool, 'discord', bot_user_id)
     ```
     If `discord_bot_user_id` is unset, fall back to decoding it from `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN` the same way `scripts/seed_channels.py:_decode_discord_user_id` does. Pass the resolved `scope.bot_id`/`scope.topic_id` into the feedback INSERT — never the reacting user's id.

## Phase 3: Dual-key rekey + per-(user,bot) pause read paths (broader survey)

### Step 10: Dual-key BurstCoalescer (`app/services/debouncer.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Extend** `_Burst` with optional `bot_id: str | None = None`.
2. **Rekey** internal dicts to composite tuple keys `(user_id, bot_id_or_None)`. New inbound passes `bot_id`; legacy/test calls without `bot_id` land on `(user_id, None)`. Lookups try the composite first, fall back to `(user_id, None)` (locked decision #1). Internal `AsyncBurstCoalescer` (third-party) remains keyed by `user_id`; bot-aware dispatch happens at our `_fire_batch` boundary.
3. **Add** `bot_id` kwarg to `BurstCoalescer.add(...)` (default `None`) and propagate into `_Burst`.
4. **Leave `# TODO(S2b): drop legacy (user_id, None) fallback`** at each fallback site.

### Step 11: Dual-key newer-inbound suppression (`app/services/agentic.py`, `app/services/tools/read_tools.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Modify** `_newer_inbound_exists` at `agentic.py:592-625`: add `AND (bot_id = $4 OR bot_id IS NULL)` to the EXISTS query, bind `ctx.bot_id` as a new parameter.
2. **Same** at `read_tools.py:152-179`.

### Step 12: Per-(user, bot) pause read path — broader survey + gateway audit (`app/services/system_state.py`, `messaging.py`, `scheduled_job_handlers.py`, `oob_check.py`, `withheld_reviews.py`, `recovery.py`, `pacer.py`, `discord.py`, `whatsapp.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `user_bot_paused(pool, user_id, bot_id) -> bool` to `system_state.py` (reads `user_bot_state.paused`).
2. **Gate** every user-facing emission/re-emission path. Sites:
   - `messaging.py:170` (`send_outbound_part`), `:335` (`send_outbound`).
   - `scheduled_job_handlers.py` dispatch before each user-facing handler fires.
   - `oob_check.py` — user-visible release.
   - `withheld_reviews.py` — released-review emissions.
   - `recovery.py:86,114` — recovery re-injects via `coalescer.add`/`add_burst`; the resulting turn goes through `send_outbound`, which gates centrally. Note `# pause-check via send_outbound` at those sites — no extra check needed.
   - `pacer.py` — paced answer/reaction emissions.
   - `discord.py`/`whatsapp.py` gateway sends: **grep-audited** in Step 1.4. All outbound emissions route through `messaging.send_outbound`/`send_outbound_part`. If any direct `INSERT INTO messages ... 'outbound'` is found in a gateway module, add the gate there too; otherwise document `# pause-check N/A: routes through send_outbound`.
3. **Audit method** for each site: grep for `send_outbound`, `send_outbound_part`, or direct outbound INSERTs. Each must either pass through a pause check OR carry a `# pause-check N/A: <reason>` comment. STOP and report if a site is structurally hard to gate.
4. **Write path stays deferred**: no code writes to `user_bot_state.paused` in S2a. Global `/pause` remains the kill switch. Document at the top of `system_state.py`.

### Step 14: Advisory linter for INSERTs (`scripts/lint_inserts.py`, CI workflow)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `scripts/lint_inserts.py` — argparse with `--help`. Walks `app/` Python files; finds SQL string literals matching `INSERT INTO (messages|bot_turns|scheduled_jobs|feedback|bridge_candidates)` and flags missing `bot_id`/`topic_id` (and `dyad_id` for `bridge_candidates`).
2. **Same** scanner flags `INSERT INTO (memories|themes|observations|watch_items|distillations|out_of_bounds)` whose enclosing SQL string lacks an `INSERT INTO artifact_topics` clause.

4. **Smoke** `python scripts/lint_inserts.py --help`. Output format: `path:line: kind: missing column 'bot_id'`.

### Step 15: Tests + fixtures (`tests/_scope_helpers.py`, new `tests/test_s2a_*.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Do NOT edit** dirty test files (`tests/conftest.py`, `tests/test_tools.py`, `tests/test_hot_context.py`, `tests/test_time_context.py`). Create `tests/_scope_helpers.py` exposing:

Tests assert both rows exist after a single `fetchrow`. No real DB.

### Step 16: Validate + branch hygiene
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** `pytest -q` — must be green. If any S1-passing test fails, STOP.
2. **Re-run** `python -m evals run --prompt-version v1` post-stamp; diff against `tests/fixtures/eval_baseline/`. Note drift in the PR description.
3. **Verify** branch hygiene: `git diff --stat <s1-tip>..HEAD -- <each dirty file>`. The 8 non-`write_tools.py` dirty files show zero changes; `write_tools.py` shows only additive INSERT-co-located changes. `docs/multi-agent-architecture.md` not committed.
4. **Verify** each `write_tools.py` commit is independently atomic (per the edit-safety contract in Step 8).

## Execution Order
1. **Phase 0** first — eval baseline in the `d4c2a7c` worktree before any code change.
2. **Phase 1** (Steps 3–5) — startup hook, `TurnContext.dyad_id`, routing wired into inbound, scope on every `TurnContext` site, `bot_turns` stamped.
3. **Phase 2** (Steps 6–9) — remaining INSERT stamping. Step 8 lands one commit per artifact CTE rewrite/extend.
4. **Phase 3** (Steps 10–12) — coalescer dual-key, suppression dual-key, pause read-path with broader survey + gateway audit.
5. **Phase 4** (Steps 13–16) — observability fan-out, linter, tests, final run + branch hygiene.

## Validation Order
1. After Step 2: `tests/fixtures/eval_baseline/` populated; worktree removed; `python scripts/capture_eval_baseline.py --help` parses.
2. After Step 5: `pytest tests/test_agentic.py tests/test_inbound_source.py tests/test_routing.py -q`.
3. After Step 8: `pytest tests/test_s2a_stamping.py tests/test_s2a_artifact_topics_same_stmt.py -q`.
4. After Step 9: bridge / feedback tests in the new file pass.
5. After Step 10: `pytest tests/test_debouncer.py tests/test_s2a_coalescer_dual_key.py -q`.
6. After Step 11: `pytest tests/test_agentic_lifecycle.py tests/test_s2a_suppression_dual_key.py -q`.
7. After Step 12: `pytest tests/test_send_outbound.py tests/test_pause_resume.py tests/test_s2a_pause_read_path.py -q`.
8. After Step 14: `python scripts/lint_inserts.py app/` — iterate until empty for new code.
9. After Step 15: full `pytest -q` green.
10. Final: `python -m evals run --prompt-version v1` diff vs baseline; Step 16 branch hygiene verified.

Reason: tier-3 cross-cutting hot-path code; finalize on Claude pre-empts the DeepSeek empty-payload failure mode we hit in S1.\n\n**Locked decisions (do not relitigate during plan/critique):**\n1. **Dual-key transition stays dual.** Coalescer and newer-inbound suppression service BOTH `(user_id)` and `(user_id, bot_id)` keys for the duration of S2a. Legacy reader paths are retired in S2b, not here.\n2. **Lint is advisory in S2a, blocking in S2b.** Warn on missing `bot_id`/`topic_id` in new INSERTs; don't fail CI yet.\n3. **Eval baseline storage**: under `tests/fixtures/eval_baseline/` with one fixture per scenario. Frozen `now_utc` and frozen DB snapshot.\n4. **Transaction discipline**: every artifact INSERT must wrap its `artifact_topics` write in the same transaction. Orphan = bug.\n5. **No NOT NULL** anywhere in this sprint. That's S2b.\n\n## Sprint 2a \u2014 \u00a716.2 verbatim\n\n**Goal.** Insert sites write the new columns; coalescer and newer-inbound suppression are rekeyed with a dual-key transition; per-bot observability comes online; pre-S3 eval baseline captured. Mediator behavior unchanged. **No NOT NULL.**\n\n**Pre-flight context.**\n\n- Confirm S1 backfill held \u2014 query `SELECT count(*) FROM mediator.artifact_topics` (must be 235 from S1; no new writes yet).\n- Exhaustive grep sweep of insert sites, broader than the obvious ones: `inbound.py:161`, `messaging.py:55, 84`, `agentic.py:483`, `agentic.py:577` (deferred jobs), `scheduled_jobs.py:216` (scheduler seeds), `write_tools.py:1367` (scheduled-task writes), every `INSERT INTO memories|themes|observations|watch_items|distillations|out_of_bounds` site. Checklist file local to the sprint.\n\n**Work items, ordered.**\n\n1. Wire `routing.py` into `inbound.py:23-180`. Populate `TurnContext` with real scope fields (still mediator-only defaults).\n2. Stamp insert sites:\n   - `inbound.py:161` \u2014 `bot_id`, `topic_id`, `channel_id`.\n   - `messaging.py:55, 84` \u2014 same on outbound.\n   - `agentic.py:483` \u2014 `bot_id`, `topic_id`, `bot_spec_version` (hash of `BotSpec` repr), `hot_context_builder_version`, `tool_schema_version` (hash of `tool_schemas.py`).\n   - `agentic.py:577` deferred jobs and `scheduled_jobs.py:216` scheduler seeds \u2014 stamp `bot_id`, `topic_id`.\n   - `write_tools.py:1367` scheduled-task writes \u2014 same.\n   - Every artifact write in `write_tools.py`: stamp `recorded_by_bot_id` AND create matching `artifact_topics` row(s) for the bot's primary topic in the same transaction.\n3. **Dual-key burst coalescer** (`app/services/debouncer.py`). In-memory dicts keyed by `user_id` today; new code services BOTH `(user_id)` and `(user_id, bot_id)` keys during this sprint. Bursts in-flight at deploy flush under old key; new bursts use the composite key. Drop the legacy reader in S2b.\n4. **Dual-key newer-inbound suppression**:\n   - `agentic.py:612` (final-send suppression).\n   - `read_tools.py:166` (incremental-send suppression).\n   - Filter: `(bot_id = ctx.bot_id OR bot_id IS NULL)` during transition \u2014 the `IS NULL` half catches legacy boundary rows written before the stamping deploy.\n5. Scheduled-job dispatcher (`scheduled_jobs.py`) reads `bot_id` from row. No-op with one bot but the code path is live.\n6. **Per-bot observability**: every log line and structured event in inbound, agentic, messaging, scheduler, OOB check carries `bot_id`, `topic_id`, `channel_id`, `binding_id`. Dashboards split by bot (even though only one bot exists yet \u2014 the panes are there for S5).\n7. **Per-(user, bot) pause read path**: outbound and scheduler consult `user_bot_state.paused` in addition to `system_state.is_paused`. Write path for per-(user, bot) pause stays deferred. Global pause remains the kill switch.\n8. **Eval baseline**: run the existing mediator eval suite against current prod-like state and snapshot results. This is the pre-S3 regression reference.\n9. Tests/fixtures take a `bot_spec` argument (default mediator).\n10. **Lint**: CI warns (does NOT fail) on any new artifact INSERT outside a transaction that also creates the `artifact_topics` row, and on any new `INSERT INTO messages|bot_turns|scheduled_jobs|feedback` missing `bot_id`/`topic_id`.\n\nDependencies: (1) gates (2\u20137); (8) runs against pre-stamp prod, so day 1.\n\n**Definition of done.**\n\n- All insert sites stamp the new columns. Coalescer + suppression rekeyed with dual-key transition live. Per-bot observability live. Per-(user, bot) pause read-path live (write-path deferred). Eval baseline captured. **No NOT NULL constraints yet.**\n- Full pytest green, no regressions.\n- Every new INSERT statement either includes `bot_id`/`topic_id` or is flagged by the lint (advisory).\n\n## S1 lessons the executor must honor\n\n1. **Before declaring any new script/CLI complete**: actually invoke it with `--help` (or equivalent entry point) to confirm it parses and basic argparse setup works. Pytest passing is necessary but not sufficient \u2014 pytest may bypass `main()`.\n2. **Any SQL fragment in a Python string**: dry-run via `EXPLAIN` against the live schema, or sanity-check the aggregate/operator types against actual column types (e.g. `max(uuid)` doesn't exist in Postgres).\n3. **Any asyncpg connection** against the configured `DATABASE_URL`: pass `statement_cache_size=0` \u2014 Supabase transaction-mode pooler (port 6543) is what's configured. Without this flag, scripts crash on first prepared-statement reuse.\n4. **No `hash()` for deterministic IDs**: Python's `hash()` is salted per-process. Use `hashlib.sha1` for advisory-lock-id derivation or anything needing cross-run determinism.\n5. **Schema filters in validation queries**: production tables live in schema `mediator`, NOT `public`. `information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = 'mediator'`.\n\n## Stop conditions\n\n- Any test that was passing on `main` starts failing \u2014 STOP, report.\n- Any work item's executor decides the design is wrong and tries to redesign \u2014 STOP, report.\n- Schema migration would touch existing rows destructively \u2014 STOP, report.\n- Any artifact INSERT path can't fit `artifact_topics` write in the same transaction \u2014 STOP, report.",

        Requirements:
        - Before addressing individual flags, check: does any flag suggest the plan is targeting the wrong code or the wrong root cause? If so, consider whether the plan needs a new approach rather than adjustments. Explain your reasoning.
        - Update the plan to address the significant issues.
        - Keep the plan readable and executable.
        - Return flags_addressed with the exact flag IDs you addressed.
        - Include `changes_summary` as a short plain-English summary of what changed in the revision. If there were no concrete flags, say that explicitly (for example: `No critique flags were raised; refined wording and kept the plan aligned for execution.`).
        - Preserve or improve success criteria quality. Each criterion must have a `priority` of `must`, `should`, or `info`. Promote or demote priorities if critique feedback reveals a criterion was over- or under-weighted.
        - Each success criterion should include a `requires` field listing the capabilities needed for verification. Valid capability strings: `run_shell`, `read_files`, `run_tests`, `parse_diff`, `read_build_output`, `run_linter` (container), `drive_browser`, `inspect_runtime_ui`, `observe_runtime_logs`, `subjective_judgment`, `verify_physical_device` (human). `must` criteria MUST have non-empty `requires`. Example: `{"criterion": "All tests pass", "priority": "must", "requires": ["run_tests"]}`.
        - Verify that the plan remains aligned with the user's original intent, not just internal plan quality.
        - Remove unjustified scope growth. If critique raised scope creep, narrow the plan back to the original idea unless the broader work is strictly required.
        - Maintain the structural template: H1 title, ## Overview, phase sections with numbered step sections, ## Execution Order or ## Validation Order.
        - CRITICAL: Your entire revised plan markdown (all sections) must be output as the `plan` field in the structured output. The prose response must not contain the plan text.
        - CRITICAL: Return only the structured JSON object for the schema fields `plan`, `changes_summary`, `flags_addressed`, `assumptions`, `success_criteria`, and `questions`. Do not add commentary before or after the JSON object.

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

104	    async def _claim_due_jobs(self, *, now: datetime, heartbeat_only: bool) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
105	        rows = await self.pool.fetch(
106	            """
107	            WITH due AS (
108	                SELECT id
109	                FROM scheduled_jobs
110	                WHERE status = 'pending'
111	                  AND scheduled_for <= $1
112	                  AND ($3::boolean = false OR job_type = 'heartbeat')
113	                  AND (claimed_at IS NULL OR claimed_at < $1::timestamptz - interval '10 minutes')
114	                ORDER BY scheduled_for ASC, created_at ASC
115	                LIMIT $2
116	                FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
117	            )
118	            UPDATE scheduled_jobs sj
119	            SET claimed_at = $1,
120	                claimed_by = $4,
121	                updated_at = $1
122	            FROM due
123	            WHERE sj.id = due.id
124	            RETURNING sj.id, sj.user_id, sj.job_type, sj.scheduled_for, sj.context,
125	                      sj.status, sj.attempt_count, sj.max_attempts, sj.delayed
126	            """,
127	            now,
128	            self.settings.scheduler_batch_size,
129	            heartbeat_only,
130	            self.worker_id,
131	        )
132	        return [dict(row) for row in rows]

Internal `AsyncBurstCoalescer` (third-party) remains keyed by `user_id`; bot-aware dispatch happens at `_fire_batch`.\n3. **Add** `bot_id` kwarg to `BurstCoalescer.add(...)` (default `None`), propagate to `_Burst`.\n4. **Mark** each fallback site with `# TODO(S2b): drop legacy (user_id, None) fallback`.\n\n### Step 12: Dual-key newer-inbound suppression (`app/services/agentic.py`, `app/services/tools/read_tools.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Modify** `_newer_inbound_exists` at `agentic.py:592-625`: add `AND (bot_id = $4 OR bot_id IS NULL)` to the EXISTS query, bind `ctx.bot_id` as a new parameter.\n2. **Same** at `read_tools.py:152-179`.\n\n### Step 13: Per-(user, bot) pause read path — broader survey + gateway audit (`app/services/system_state.py`, `messaging.py`, `scheduled_job_handlers.py`, `oob_check.py`, `withheld_reviews.py`, `recovery.py`, `pacer.py`, `discord.py`, `whatsapp.py`, `hooks.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Add** `user_bot_paused(pool, user_id, bot_id) -> bool` in `system_state.py` (reads `user_bot_state.paused`).\n2. **Add** an optional `bot_id` kwarg to `hooks.paused_for_user` (`app/services/hooks.py:37`). Callers in `messaging.py:170,335` pass `ctx.bot_id`. Internal implementation may treat `None` as global pause only — but having the parameter is the readiness step for S2b.\n3. **Gate sites:**\n   - `messaging.py:170` (`send_outbound_part`), `:335` (`send_outbound`): withhold if `is_paused OR user_bot_paused(pool, user.id, bot_id)`.\n   - `scheduled_job_handlers.py` dispatch (`scheduled_jobs.py:134`-side or in each handler's entry): gate before fire.\n   - `oob_check.py`: user-visible release.\n   - `withheld_reviews.py`: released-review emissions.\n   - `recovery.py:86,114`: re-injects via `coalescer.add` / `add_burst`; downstream turn goes through `send_outbound` → gated centrally. Annotate with `# pause-check via send_outbound`.\n   - `pacer.py`: paced answer/reaction emissions.\n   - `discord.py`/`whatsapp.py`: grep-confirmed all outbound routes through `messaging.send_outbound`/`send_outbound_part`. Annotate with `# pause-check N/A: routes through send_outbound`.\n4. **Audit method**: grep each file for `send_outbound`, `send_outbound_part`, or direct outbound INSERTs. Each site either passes a gate OR carries a `# pause-check N/A: <reason>` comment. STOP if structurally hard.\n5. **Write path stays deferred**: no code writes to `user_bot_state.paused` in S2a. Document at the top of `system_state.py`.\n\n## Phase 4: Observability, lint, tests, validation\n\n### Step 14: Per-bot observability fields (`app/services/turn_context.py`, then enumerated emitter files)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Add** `obs_fields(ctx_or_scope) -> dict` to `app/services/turn_context.py` returning `{\"bot_id\": ..., \"topic_id\": ..., \"channel_id\": ..., \"binding_id\": ...}` (None values filtered).\n2. **Hot-path** `logger.*` calls pass `extra=obs_fields(ctx)`:\n   - `inbound.py`, `agentic.py`, `messaging.py`, `scheduled_job_handlers.py`, `oob_check.py`, `turn_audit.py`.\n3. **Adjacent emitters** with concrete line ranges:\n   - `discord.py:107-110` (`send_text`), `:115-122` (`add_reaction`), `:129-130` (edit), `:137-138` (delete), `:153-156` (`send_typing`), `:411-421` (gateway message handler), `:450-478` (reaction handler), `:481-522` (`catch_up_recent_messages`). Where no `ctx` exists, build scope from local `bot_id`/`channel_id` or call `_resolve_scope` once at function top.\n   - `whatsapp.py` (~150 lines): apply `extra=` to every `logger.*` site.\n   - `hooks.py` (40 lines): `paused_for_user` (`:37`) logs `extra={\"user_id\": user_id, \"bot_id\": bot_id}` (uses the new kwarg from Step 13.2); `_default_check_oob` (`:13-28`) logs `extra={\"user_id\": recipient_id}` if it logs at all.\n   - `withheld_reviews.py`: thread `bot_id`/`topic_id` from the caller (Step 10.3) into both the row INSERT (column write deferred to S2b) and any `logger.*` calls' `extra=`.\n   For sites with **no scope context** (transport-only connection errors), skip and add `# obs N/A: transport-only`.\n4. **`record_turn_event` callers** (`agentic.py`, `turn_audit.py`): `metadata` dict carries `bot_id`/`topic_id`/`channel_id`/`binding_id`. Function shape unchanged.\n5. **Note** in `s2a_preflight.md`: dashboard panes are downstream config, out of scope.\n\n### Step 15: Advisory linter for INSERTs (`scripts/lint_inserts.py`, CI workflow)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Implement** `scripts/lint_inserts.py` — argparse `--help`. Walks `app/` files; scans SQL string literals matching `INSERT INTO (messages|bot_turns|scheduled_jobs|feedback|bridge_candidates)`; flags missing `bot_id`/`topic_id` (and `dyad_id` for `bridge_candidates`).\n2. **Same** scanner flags `INSERT INTO (memories|themes|observations|watch_items|distillations|out_of_bounds)` whose enclosing SQL string lacks an `INSERT INTO artifact_topics` clause (substring check, not structural).\n3. **CI step**: `lint-inserts` runs `python scripts/lint_inserts.py || true` — exit always 0 in S2a. `# TODO(S2b): make blocking` comment in the script.\n4. **Smoke** `python scripts/lint_inserts.py --help`. Output: `path:line: kind: missing column 'bot_id'`.\n\n### Step 16: Tests + fixtures (`tests/_scope_helpers.py`, new `tests/test_s2a_*.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Do NOT edit** dirty test files. Create `tests/_scope_helpers.py` exposing:\n   - `make_mediator_ctx(bot_spec=None, **overrides)` building a `TurnContext` with mediator defaults including `dyad_id`.\n   - `make_resolved_scope(**overrides)` mock `ResolvedScope`.\n   - **`StampingFakePool(FakePool)`** subclass — **substring matching only**, not structural SQL parsing. Pseudocode:\n     ```python\n     class StampingFakePool(FakePool):\n         def __init__(self):\n             super().__init__()\n             self._artifacts: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}\n             self._artifact_topics: list[dict] = []\n\n         async def fetchrow(self, sql, *args):\n             # Substring detection — does NOT require exact CTE wording.\n             for table in (\"memories\", \"themes\", \"observations\",\n                           \"watch_items\", \"distillations\", \"out_of_bounds\"):\n                 if f\"INSERT INTO {table}\" in sql:\n                     row = {\"id\": uuid4(), \"args\": args}\n                     self._artifacts.setdefault(table, []).append(row)\n                     if \"INSERT INTO artifact_topics\" in sql:\n                         self._artifact_topics.append({\"artifact_table\": table,\n                                                       \"artifact_id\": row[\"id\"]})\n                     return row\n             return await super().fetchrow(sql, *args)\n     ```\n2. **Add** new test files:\n   - `tests/test_s2a_stamping.py` — verifies `messages`/`bot_turns`/`scheduled_jobs`/`feedback`/`bridge_candidates` stamps including `bot_spec_version`/`hot_context_builder_version`/`tool_schema_version` (sha1, deterministic across two process runs) and `bridge_candidates.dyad_id`.\n   - `tests/test_s2a_artifact_topics_same_stmt.py` — every artifact write produces an `artifact_topics` row via `StampingFakePool`.\n   - `tests/test_s2a_coalescer_dual_key.py` — `(user_id, bot_id)` and `(user_id, None)` keys flush correctly.\n   - `tests/test_s2a_suppression_dual_key.py` — EXISTS query includes `(bot_id = $ OR bot_id IS NULL)`.\n   - `tests/test_s2a_pause_read_path.py` — `user_bot_state.paused=TRUE` withholds at messaging, scheduler dispatch, and at least one re-emission site (`withheld_reviews` or `pacer`).\n   - `tests/test_s2a_scheduler_dispatch_bot_id.py` — instrumented handler asserts `job[\"bot_id\"]` populated; checks the claim-query RETURNING list contains `bot_id, topic_id`.\n   - `tests/test_s2a_discord_id_helper.py` — `discord_bot_user_id()` returns env var when set, falls back to decoded token, returns None when both empty. Confirms `_decode_discord_user_id` parity with the S1 seeder.\n   - `tests/test_s2a_lint_inserts.py` — invokes `scripts/lint_inserts.py` against fixture strings.\n\n### Step 17: Validate + branch hygiene\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Run** `pytest -q` — green. If any S1-passing test fails, STOP.\n2. **Re-run** `python -m evals run --prompt-version v1`; diff against `tests/fixtures/eval_baseline/`. Note drift in the PR description.\n3. **Branch hygiene**: `git diff --stat <s1-tip>..HEAD -- <each dirty file>`. The 8 non-`write_tools.py` dirty files show zero changes; `write_tools.py` shows only additive INSERT-localized changes. `docs/multi-agent-architecture.md` not committed.\n4. **Confirm** each `write_tools.py` commit is independently atomic.\n\n## Execution Order\n1. **Phase 0** first — eval baseline in the `d4c2a7c` worktree before any code change.\n2. **Phase 1** (Steps 3–6) — discord_id helper, startup hook, `TurnContext.dyad_id`, routing wired into inbound, scope on every `TurnContext` site, `bot_turns` stamped.\n3. **Phase 2** (Steps 7–10) — outbound, scheduler (claim query + handlers + seeds), artifacts (one commit per CTE rewrite/extend), bridge/feedback/withheld_reviews kwargs.\n4. **Phase 3** (Steps 11–13) — coalescer dual-key, suppression dual-key, pause read-path with broader survey + gateway audit + `hooks.paused_for_user(bot_id=...)`.\n5. **Phase 4** (Steps 14–17) — observability fan-out, linter, tests, final run + branch hygiene.\n\n## Validation Order\n1. After Step 2: `tests/fixtures/eval_baseline/` populated; worktree removed; `python scripts/capture_eval_baseline.py --help` parses.\n2. After Step 3: `pytest tests/test_s2a_discord_id_helper.py -q` (or focused new test); the seeder script's import still works (`python scripts/seed_channels.py --help` if argparse, otherwise import-only smoke).\n3. After Step 6: `pytest tests/test_agentic.py tests/test_inbound_source.py tests/test_routing.py -q`.\n4. After Step 9: `pytest tests/test_s2a_stamping.py tests/test_s2a_artifact_topics_same_stmt.py -q`.\n5. After Step 10: bridge / feedback / withheld_reviews tests pass.\n6. After Step 11: `pytest tests/test_debouncer.py tests/test_s2a_coalescer_dual_key.py -q`.\n7. After Step 12: `pytest tests/test_agentic_lifecycle.py tests/test_s2a_suppression_dual_key.py -q`.\n8.
